Yet their probabilistic nature makes them, in isolation, fundamentally unsuited for industrial product configuration, where outputs must be syntactically valid, semantically consistent with a knowledge base of hundreds of features and rules, and producible by an existing manufacturing chain. We argue that Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI methods lay out a promising path towards industrial-grade configurators that are reliable by design, explainable, and trustworthy. This paper describes a taxonomy of three NeSy integration strategies, namely hybrid inference, hybrid fine-tuning, and hybrid training, exploring their usage in the configuration domain. We report our effort to operationalize NeSy concepts in an industrial configuration copilot and derive a set of practical design choices for deploying trustworthy AI in engineering environments. We close with a discussion of open research challenges we consider most pressing, in particular how to scale NeSy methods from small academic demonstrators to the size of industrial configurators.

</details>


### [194] [ENDOPROMPT: Victim-Side Pseudo-References for Utility Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29948)
*Qingyu Wu,Zeyu Feng,Yongda Yu,Yuzhe Luo,Hua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ENDOPROMPT，一种白盒方法，通过学习能降低模型效用的前缀来攻击指令微调模型，无需依赖任务标签或有害内容，仅从未标注指令中学习。该方法在4个指令微调模型和7个良性基准的完整划分上平均效用下降26.8个百分点，其中27/28个单元格为负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示注入攻击大多依赖任务标签或预定义的目标响应，而实际上效用退化（utility degradation）可以在不产生有害内容的情况下破坏良性任务性能。作者希望从受害者自身监督信号中发现模型弱点，无需基准反馈或预设的失败响应，从而更全面地评估模型鲁棒性。

Method: ENDOPROMPT的生成器以请求文本为输入，利用干净的受害者续写结果作为伪参考。通过局部搜索（local search）识别降低续写似然度的前缀，再在同一指令内的比较上进行偏好拟合（preference fitting），随后进行奖励细化（reward refinement），将此信号蒸馏到生成器中。部署时，生成器对每个请求直接产生前缀，无需在受害者侧进行额外搜索。

Result: 在四个指令微调模型和七个良性基准的完整划分上，ENDOPROMPT实现了平均26.8个百分点的效用变化，27/28个单元格为负值。失败分析揭示了输出扩张（output expansion）和前缀复用（prefix reuse）现象；对照组未能证明请求匹配（request matching）能带来效用退化优势。

Conclusion: 受害者派生的监督信号可以在无需基准反馈或预设失败响应的情况下揭示模型效用弱点。ENDOPROMPT证明了纯效用退化型攻击的有效性与泛化性，代码将在论文被接收后开源。

Abstract: Prompt injection can degrade benign task performance without eliciting harmful content. Yet many attack objectives depend on task labels or predefined target responses. We present ENDOPROMPT, a white-box method that learns utility-degrading prefixes from unlabeled instructions. Its generator takes the request text as input. Clean victim continuations serve as pseudo-references: local search identifies prefixes that reduce continuation likelihood, and preference fitting on comparisons within the same instruction, followed by reward refinement, distills this signal into a generator. At deployment, the generator produces one prefix per request without further victim-side search. Across four instruction-tuned models and the complete splits of seven benign benchmarks, ENDOPROMPT yields a mean utility change of -26.8 percentage points; 27 of 28 cells are negative. Failure analysis reveals output expansion and prefix reuse; the controls do not establish a degradation advantage from request matching. Victim-derived supervision can reveal utility weaknesses without benchmark feedback or prescribed failure responses. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [195] [Augur: A Synthetic Decision Lab for Rehearsing Reactions to Product and Policy Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29952)
*Rahul Khedar,Mayank Malhotra,Avinash Karn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Augur——一个离线模拟公众对产品或政策变更反应的系统，它通过构建类型化知识图谱、建立基于角色的市场并模拟交互来生成审计备忘录。核心发现是方法学层面的负面结论：前沿云模型与离线微调的开源模型之间的多数性能差距，源于评估规范不足而非能力差异。通过在Qwen3-32B上的LoRA-SFT适配器实验、2x2配对消融以及蒸馏一致性分析，论文从三个角度论证了这一结论。此外，反应层验证实验表明合成反应能恢复67-90%的公众关切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在产品或政策变更上线前，关键问题是人们会如何反应。研究者希望构建一个离线框架来预演这种反应，但目前对模型评估方法本身缺乏严谨性——特别是前沿模型与开源微调模型之间的性能差异可能被不当评估所混淆。

Method: Augur框架包含四个步骤：从变更文档构建类型化知识图谱、构建基于角色的市场、模拟交互并输出审计决策备忘单（推荐五种行动之一）。研究者构建了Gold-50数据集（50个真实产品/政策事件），并通过三种方式论证评估不充分的问题：1）提示词包装对分数的影响实验；2）匹配2x2消融实验（在提示词中定义决策分类法）；3）与蒸馏教师模型的一致性分析。反应层通过四个模型家族的盲审验证，并使用预注册消融评估其价值。

Result: 1）提示词包装效应：固定权重、案例和评分器，同一Qwen3-32B LoRA-SFT适配器得分从0%到73%之间摆动；2）2x2消融：在提示词中定义决策分类法使每个前沿模型提升+24至+34个百分点；在欠规范提示下，Qwen3-32B LoRA-SFT离线服务胜过三个前沿模型，但提示词公平后无显著差异；3）蒸馏一致性上升但准确性未随之提升，完整流程放大了"过度悲观"偏差；4）反应层：合成反应恢复67-90%的公众关切，价值在决策最困难处最大。

Conclusion: 结论是：当前衡量模型在真实决策任务上表现的方式存在系统性缺陷——评估规范不足（特别是决策分类法的定义方式）对分数的影响远超模型本身的能力差异。研究者强调需要更严格的评估协议，并指出蒸馏方法可能放大系统偏差。

Abstract: Before a product or policy change ships, the question that matters is how people will react to it. Augur rehearses that reaction offline: it builds a typed knowledge graph from the change documents, populates a grounded persona market, simulates the interaction, and returns an auditable decision memo recommending one of five actions. We assemble Gold-50, fifty real product and policy episodes whose real-world outcome is known, adjudicated against the public record, and score the five-way release verdict against it.
  Our central finding is methodological and negative: most of the measured gap between frontier cloud models and open-weight models we fine-tune and serve offline is attributable to an under-specified evaluation, not a difference in capability. We show this three ways. First, the prompt envelope alone can dominate the score: holding weights, cases and scorer fixed, one system -- a LoRA-SFT adapter on Qwen3-32B -- swings from 0% to 73%. Second, in a matched 2x2 ablation, defining the decision taxonomy in the prompt -- with no model change -- lifts every frontier model by +24 to +34pp; under the under-specified prompt, Qwen3-32B LoRA-SFT served offline beats all three frontier models (paired McNemar, Holm-corrected), and once the prompt is fair no significant difference from any of them is detected. Third, agreement with the distillation teacher rises without accuracy following, and the full pipeline amplifies a systematic "over-doom" bias rather than improving the verdict. Separately, we validate the reaction layer on its own terms: blind judges across four model families find the synthetic reaction recovers 67-90% of the concerns the public actually raised, and a pre-registered ablation locates its value -- largest where the decision is hardest, redundant near ceiling. The pipeline that regenerates every number and figure here is available from the authors.

</details>


### [196] [Advancing Model Research in AgentX: Long-Horizon Autonomy for Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30001)
*Shuang Yang,Zijie Zhuang,Changxin Lao,Pengbo Xu,Hanwen Xu,Yusheng Huang,Han Gao,Guanchen Wang,Tianbao Ma,Linxun Chen,Peilin Song,Xuming Wang,Chen Li,Fan Wu,Tao Wang,Zibo Zhao,Xiangyu Wu,An Liu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Chen Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AgentX-Model，一个用于工业推荐研究的两阶段代理框架。该框架采用双代理架构（Research Agent和Model Agent），通过四个核心动作（复现、跟进、组合、诊断）组织持续研究。在636个完成的模型改动实验中，560个的AUC超过业务基线，最新5次在线A/B评估在不同业务场景中报告了显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续的工业推荐研究需要利用一次实验结果来指导下一次研究决策，而非孤立地开展实验。需要一个系统化的框架来连接提案开发与模型实验，使后续实验能够基于先前发现持续累积。

Method: 采用双代理架构：Research Agent负责从论文和实验结果中开发经过独立评审的提案；Model Agent执行多轮调研并返回代码、测量数据和未解决问题。围绕四个动作组织研究流程：Reproduce（复现）、Follow-up（跟进）、Composition（组合）和Diagnose（诊断），其中Diagnose用于获取修复所需的证据（如PCOC预测偏差）。

Result: 在636个完成的模型改动实验中，560个AUC超过业务基线；部分实验在其血统中优于所有可比较的祖先实验。最新5次在线A/B评估报告了不同业务场景的收益：获取效率提升10-15%，目标细分广告支出提升15-20%，观看时间提升0.3-0.8%且模型使用约少10%的FLOPs和参数。依赖感知的历史回放基准初始结果显示，在代理已能分析并选择具体候选方案时，更复杂的调度并未带来一致的效率增益。

Conclusion: AgentX-Model通过将研究过程组织为可重复的代理驱动循环，有效提升了工业推荐研究的可持续性和累积性。离线与在线评估均验证了其有效性，同时回放基准提示在现有代理能力下，简单的调度策略就已足够。

Abstract: Sustaining industrial recommendation research requires using the results of one experiment to decide what to investigate next. We present AgentX-Model, the next generation of AgentX's model research framework, which connects proposal development and model experimentation within sandboxes defined by business inputs and prediction tasks. AgentX-Model adopts a dual-agent architecture comprising a Research Agent and a Model Agent. The Research Agent develops independently reviewed proposals from papers and experimental findings, while the Model Agent conducts multi-round investigations and returns code, measurements, and unresolved questions. Using the returned results, the Research Agent selects a starting implementation and formulates the next research question, allowing subsequent experiments to build on earlier findings. We organize this continuing research around four actions: Reproduce, Follow-up, Composition, and Diagnose. The first three actions drive routine research, while Diagnose acquires the evidence needed to choose a repair, including for issues raised by business feedback and online evaluation, such as prediction bias measured by PCOC. Across the production evaluation, 560 of 636 completed model-changing experiments recorded AUC above their business baselines. As research continued, some experiments recorded AUC above every comparable ancestor in their lineages. The five latest online A/B evaluations across different business settings reported gains including 10-15% in acquisition efficiency, 15-20% in target-segment advertising spend, and 0.3-0.8% in watch time; the watch-time model used approximately 10% fewer FLOPs and parameters. A dependency-aware historical-replay benchmark further evaluates research allocation, with initial results showing no consistent efficiency gain from more complex scheduling when agents already analyze and select concrete candidates.

</details>


### [197] [Synthetic Hospital: An Open, Verifiable, Physician-Validated Longitudinal EHR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30027)
*Christine Park,Valerie Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Synthetic Hospital，一个完全公开、合成、基于事实的纵向电子健康记录（EHR）基准数据集。它由纯公共医学教育材料构建（不含受保护健康信息），包含1,268名纵向患者和5,602次就诊，所有诊断、发现和时间关系均基于标准本体（ICD-10-CM、SNOMED CT、LOINC）并带有完整溯源链。通过模拟医院记录系统提供服务（标准互操作API、基于角色的访问和函数调用接口）。盲审中医生识别其与真实病历的准确率接近随机水平（53%）。在10个前沿与开源模型中，最佳模型重建患者纵向问题清单的严重度加权F1为0.73，与7名医生的平均值持平但远低于最佳医生（0.89），且在总结病历时遗漏约一半的临床相关发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型很少用于临床工作流程，因为开发它们所需的现实、纵向基准数据稀缺。真实EHR数据因隐私、伦理或数据使用问题无法公开共享，且由于病历仅反映临床医生所记录的内容，其中不包含可验证的ground truth。为克服这一障碍，需要构建一个既能公开共享又具有可验证事实依据的纵向EHR基准。

Method: 作者完全从公开的医学教育材料构建Synthetic Hospital基准，不包含任何受保护健康信息。每个诊断、发现和时间关系均锚定于标准本体（ICD-10-CM、SNOMED CT、LOINC），并带有回溯源医学教育材料的完整溯源链。该基准通过一个模拟医院记录系统提供服务，该系统镜像真实EHR基础设施，包括标准互操作API、基于角色的访问和函数调用接口。

Result: 盲审中，医生识别其记录与真实患者病历的准确率接近随机水平（53%）。在10个前沿及开源模型中，没有模型接近上限：最佳模型重建患者纵向问题清单的严重度加权F1为0.73，与匹配子集上7名医生的平均值持平，但远低于最佳医生（0.89）；在总结病历时会遗漏约一半的临床相关发现。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，Synthetic Hospital是评估临床AI性能的一个困难和现实的测试基准，既解决了真实EHR数据无法公开共享和缺乏可验证ground truth的两大障碍，又通过盲审和模型性能验证了其逼真性和挑战性。

Abstract: Frontier language models are rarely used in clinical workflows because the realistic, longitudinal benchmarks needed to develop them are scarce. Real electronic health record (EHR) data cannot be openly shared due to privacy, ethics or data use issues and it does not contain verifiable ground truth since the chart records only reflect what clinicians documented. We introduce Synthetic Hospital, an open, fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark that resolves the open sharing and verifiable ground truth barriers. Built entirely from public medical-education material with no protected health information, it comprises 1,268 longitudinal patients and 5,602 encounters, where every diagnosis, finding, and temporal relation is grounded in standard ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and with a complete provenance chain back to its source medical education material. Synthetic Hospital is served through a simulated hospital record system that mirrors real EHR infrastructure (standard interoperability APIs, role-based access and function-calling interface). In a blinded review, physicians distinguished its records from real patient charts at near-chance rates (53\%). Across 10 frontier and open models, none approaches ceiling: the best model reconstructs a patient's longitudinal problem list with a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73, level with the mean of seven physicians on a matched subset but well below the best of them (0.89), and misses roughly half of clinically relevant findings when summarizing a chart. Overall, these results highlight that Synthetic Hospital is a difficult and realistic test of clinical AI performance.

</details>


### [198] [How does Adversarial Influence Scale in Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30028)
*Addison J. Wu,Jasin Cekinmez,Michel Liao,Karthik Narasimhan,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体群体中欺骗行为对整体决策的影响，发现智能体对欺骗的易感性取决于欺骗者比例而非群体规模，且语言模型智能体即使在欺骗者为少数时也会频繁背叛正确结论，增加智能体数量并不能有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作可提升性能，但现实场景中存在不诚信智能体（受自身目标或外部指令驱动）可能破坏群体共识。以往研究多关注协作收益，缺乏对群体规模、欺骗者比例等条件下系统性脆弱性的量化分析。

Method: 通过控制群体中诚实智能体与欺骗性智能体的比例、群体大小及模型类型，进行大规模实验。测量“背叛率”（初始正确但最终转向错误答案的频率），并比较不同模型组合下的表现，还考察欺骗者私下协调的影响。

Result: 背叛率随欺骗者比例线性上升，与群体绝对规模无关；语言模型智能体在欺骗者仅占少数时也会经常背叛，而人类只在误导性同盟占多数时才显著动摇；易感性主要取决于交互的模型类型（尤其是诚实智能体一侧）；允许欺骗者私下协调反而降低其有效性。

Conclusion: 单纯增加智能体数量不是可靠防御手段，因为对手可同步扩编。防御需关注模型选择、比例阈值及协调机制，而非仅依赖群体规模。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation can improve performance, but what happens when some agents do not act in good faith? In practice, an agent may be deceptive and work to subvert the group, whether through its own objectives or external instruction. We study how susceptibility to deception scales as groups increase in size and deceivers become more prevalent. It is not the number of agents in the group that matters, but the proportion of deceivers. We observe that the defection rate, how often initially correct agents switch to an incorrect final answer, rises linearly with this proportion. Whereas humans in comparable conformity studies are reliably swayed only when misleading confederates form a majority, LLM agents defect regularly even when deceivers remain a minority. Susceptibility also depends on which models are interacting, especially on the honest agent side. Unexpectedly, allowing deceivers to coordinate privately can make them less effective. Altogether, our results show that adding more agents is therefore not a sufficient defense, because the adversary can simply scale with the group.

</details>


### [199] [Style, Not Self: Surface Cues Explain Zero-Shot Code Attribution by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30048)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Surendrabikram Thapa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型是否偏爱自己生成的代码。15种模型-基准组合下，模型在零样本条件下识别自己代码的平衡准确率仅为49-58%，接近随机水平。原始准确率高低主要反映模型声称作者身份的倾向。通过规则化预处理（去除文档字符串、注释、类型提示和局部变量名）后，十二个重测结果中有十个降至随机水平。作者建议报告平衡准确率、启发式基线和标签一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果语言模型能识别自己写的代码，那么它在作为评判者时可能会偏好自己的代码，导致模型之间相互监控时可能产生共谋（collusion）。这是一个关于AI系统安全性、公正性和问责制的潜在风险问题，需要实证检验。

Method: 在零样本条件下测试当前商业模型。五个LLM生成MBPP、HumanEval和DS-1000的解决方案，另外七个模型生成MBPP方案。模型作为评估者参与四个任务：(1) 从一对解中选出自己的解；(2) 判断单个解是否自己写的；(3) 识别两个解中哪个由指定模型编写；(4) 盲评质量。同时进行规则化归一化预处理（去除docstrings、注释、类型提示、局部名）并重新测试，还训练了分类器验证归一化后的可分离性。

Result: 单解任务中，所有15个模型-基准组合的平衡准确率为49-58%，原始准确率（38-67%）主要反映模型声明作者身份的倾向。成对任务中，14个评估者-对手组合的准确率与评估者解法更长的频率相关性为r=0.93。命名模型归因在某些组合上成功、某些组合上系统性反转。规则化归一化保留Pass@1且使十二个重测结果中十个降至随机水平；剩下两个受长度差异影响。Claude Haiku的自偏好也消失了。

Conclusion: 当前商业语言模型在零样本条件下并不能可靠地识别自己生成的代码，自偏好主要源于表面特征（如长度、文档格式等），而非深层代码风格。通过简单的规则化归一化即可消除多数自偏好，说明模型的自偏好是可解释的表层启发式。建议评估和报告此类能力时使用平衡准确率、启发式基线和标签一致性。

Abstract: If a language model can recognize code it wrote, it may favor that code as a judge, and instances of one model monitoring each other could collude. We test this zero-shot on current commercial models. Five LLMs generate solutions to MBPP, HumanEval, and DS-1000, seven more to MBPP, and models act as evaluators in four tasks: picking their own solution from a pair, judging whether a single solution is their own, identifying which of two solutions a named model wrote, and judging quality blind. In the single-solution task, balanced accuracy is 49-58% for all 15 model-benchmark combinations, while raw accuracy (38-67%) mostly reflects how readily a model claims authorship. In the pairwise task, accuracy across 14 evaluator-opponent combinations correlates at r=0.93 with how often the evaluator's solution is longer. Attribution to a named model succeeds on some pairs and is consistently inverted on others. A rule-based normalization that strips docstrings, comments, type hints, and local names preserves Pass@1 and leaves ten of twelve re-tested results at chance; the other two follow a length difference it leaves, although a trained classifier still separates most normalized pairs. Claude Haiku's self-preference also disappears. We recommend reporting balanced accuracy, heuristic baselines, and label consistency.

</details>


### [200] [NNV3: Expanding Neural Network Verification to New Architectures and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30050)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Samuel Sasaki,Ben Wooding,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Muhammad Usama Zubair,Navid Hashemi,Hongchao Zhang,Waseem Abbas,Ipek Oguz,Meiyi Ma,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NNV3，即神经网络验证工具NNV的最新版本。NNV3在原有基于集合可达性分析的框架（支持前馈神经网络、卷积神经网络、神经网络控制系统的1.0版本，以及循环神经网络、语义分割神经网络、神经常微分方程的2.0版本）基础上，新增了Star-set家族成员：ModelStar（验证权重扰动下的网络）、VolumeStar（处理视频和三维体积输入）以及GraphStar（支持图神经网络）。同时引入基于共形推断的概率可达性模式，用于处理确定性验证不可行的问题；FairNNV用于在连续输入区域上认证反事实公平和个体公平属性。NNV3还新增了恶意软件检测、图基电力系统模型、医学影像、变长时序数据和动作识别等基准测试，并通过统一文档站点提供教程和开发者指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型和智能网络物理系统在安全关键领域的广泛应用，形式化验证变得至关重要。然而现有验证工具在拓展性（支持更多网络类型）、输入多样性（视频、三维体积、图数据）以及实际问题（权重扰动、公平性、概率验证）方面存在局限。NNV3旨在将NNV发展为涵盖更多AI系统、具备全面功能、稳健且易用的综合性验证工具。

Method: NNV3以NNV 1.0和2.0的基于集合的可达性方法为基础，扩展了Star-set家族以支持更多数据形态和网络结构：ModelStar处理权重扰动下的网络验证，VolumeStar支持视频和三维体积输入，GraphStar支持图神经网络。新增基于共形推断的概率可达性模式作为确定性验证不可行时的补充。FairNNV模块在连续输入区域上对反事实公平和个体公平性进行认证。同时引入覆盖多种应用领域的新基准测试，并通过统一文档站点集成教程和开发者指南。

Result: NNV3成功实现了向涵盖多种AI系统的综合、稳健、易用验证工具的成熟化转变。其新增的Star-set成员扩展了工具对网络类型和输入形态的支持能力，概率可达性模式与确定性验证形成互补，FairNNV提供了公平性验证能力，新增基准测试覆盖恶意软件检测、电力系统图模型、医学影像、变长时序数据和动作识别等多样化领域。

Conclusion: NNV3标志着NNV工具已成熟为针对多样化AI系统的全面、稳健且易访问的验证平台。通过引入新的Star-set成员、概率验证模式、公平性认证以及多样化基准和文档支持，NNV3显著拓展了形式化验证在深度学习及智能网络物理系统中的应用版图。

Abstract: We present NNV3, the latest version of the Neural Network Verification (NNV) tool, a MATLAB framework for formal verification of deep learning models and learning-enabled cyber-physical systems. Building on the set-based reachability foundation of NNV 1.0 (FFNNs, CNNs, NNCS) and NNV 2.0 (RNNs, SSNNs, neural ODEs), NNV3 introduces new members of the Star-set family: ModelStar for verifying networks under weight perturbation, VolumeStar for video and 3D volumetric inputs, and GraphStar for graph neural networks. A conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode complements sound analysis for problems where deterministic verification is intractable, while FairNNV certifies counterfactual and individual fairness properties over continuous input regions. NNV3 introduces new benchmarks for malware detection, graph-based power-system models, medical imaging, variable-length time series data, and action recognition. NNV3 also incorporates tutorials and developer guides through a unified documentation site. This paper details these major updates, demonstrating NNV's maturation into a comprehensive, robust, and accessible verification tool for a diverse range of AI systems.

</details>


### [201] [SciWalker: Synthesizing Scientific Coding Problems with Operator Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30054)
*Chenxi Li,Wenxuan Zeng,Yun Luo,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Yu Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciWalker是一个通过算子链采样和执行反馈来合成科学编程问题的框架，构建了涵盖5个科学领域32个子领域的8,178个高质量问题，并通过GSPO算法对Qwen3.5-9B进行强化学习训练，将SciCode子问题准确率从29.3%提升至39.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的科学编码能力提升依赖高质量训练数据，但人工编写真实科学问题成本高且耗时，同时系统性覆盖多样化科学领域和算法组合具有挑战性，因此需要一种可扩展的自动化数据合成方法。

Method: SciWalker框架将科学库接口与操作模式结合以实例化算子，组织成算子图并采样算子链作为计算工作流线索；在此基础上使用LLM生成科学问题陈述、参考解决方案和测试用例，并用执行反馈迭代修复失败的生成结果；最后通过结构化工作流组合、验证和质量审查确保科学性、多样性和可执行性。

Result: 构建了8,178个高质量科学编程问题，覆盖5个科学领域和32个子领域；使用GSPO算法对Qwen3.5-9B进行强化学习训练后，SciCode子问题准确率从29.3%提升至39.2%（提升9.9个百分点），并在科学代码生成、代码修复和推理基准上均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过算子链采样与执行反馈相结合的方式，SciWalker实现了科学编程问题的大规模自动生成，验证了结构化工作流组合结合验证与质量审查能够有效提升训练数据的科学性、多样性和可执行性，从而显著增强LLM的科学编码能力。

Abstract: Improving the scientific coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality training data. However, such data remain scarce because manually authoring realistic problems is costly and time-consuming, while systematically covering diverse scientific domains and algorithmic combinations remains challenging. To address this, we introduce SciWalker, a framework for synthesizing scientific coding problems through operator-chain sampling and execution feedback. The framework combines scientific library interfaces with operation modes to instantiate operators, organizes them into operator graphs, and samples operator chains as computational workflow cues. Guided by these cues, we adopt LLMs to generate scientifically grounded problem statements, reference solutions, and tests, with failed generations iteratively repaired using execution feedback. By combining structured workflow composition with verification and quality review, SciWalker enables scalable task generation while promoting scientific grounding, computational diversity, and executability. Using this framework, we construct 8,178 high-quality problems spanning 5 scientific domains and 32 subdomains. To evaluate their training utility, we conduct reinforcement learning on Qwen3.5-9B using the GSPO algorithm. This training improves SciCode subproblem accuracy by 9.9 percentage points, from 29.3% to 39.2%, with gains across scientific code generation, code repair, and reasoning benchmarks. The code for SciWalker is available at https://github.com/lichenx1/SciWalker.

</details>


### [202] [Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30063)
*Aditya Cowsik,Kfir Dolev,Michael Y. Li,G. Bruno De Luca,Nourya Cohen,Noah D. Goodman,Yoav Levine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"零数据自博弈预训练"（Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data），一种无需任何自然数据即可预训练语言模型的初步概念验证。该方法将合成数据生成视为对所有可计算结构的搜索，受Solomonoff归纳启发：两个模型（生成器和学习器）协同训练，生成器通过强化学习在被通用图灵机解释的程序空间中搜索并生成字节序列，学习器则用标准交叉熵自回归预测这些序列。实验表明，在多个自然数据集上，零样本损失随自博弈计算量呈现可预测的缩放规律，模型还展现出上下文学习能力并能发现可识别的数学序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的预训练数据仍由人工精心策划，限制了训练数据的规模和多样性。作者希望让模型自己学习生成对自身改进最有用的数据，从而摆脱对人类知识的依赖，实现由计算而非数据驱动的无限训练数据来源。

Method: 受Solomonoff归纳启发的自博弈框架：生成器提出由通用图灵机解释的程序，产生字节序列；学习器用自回归方式预测这些序列。生成器通过强化学习（REINFORCE类算法）训练，目标是生成处于学习器能力前沿的序列，形成自适应课程学习。整个过程从随机初始化开始，不接触任何自然数据。

Result: 在多个自然语言数据集上，零样本损失随自博弈计算量呈可预测的幂律缩放。模型表现出上下文学习能力，且在训练过程中能够自发发现可识别的数学序列（如Fibonacci数等）。这证明了从合成数据到自然数据的迁移是可能的且可预测的。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个初步概念验证，表明完全由模型自我生成数据驱动的预训练是可行的，且性能提升可预测。虽然目前仍是初步成果，但为"以计算换数据"的预训练范式开辟了新方向，有望突破人类知识对模型规模扩大的限制。

Abstract: Advances in language modeling have been driven by scaling pretraining on ever more data. Yet, the training data is still largely curated on the model's behalf. A more general approach to pretraining would let the model learn to generate the data most useful for its own improvement. This would provide an effectively unbounded source of training data, limited by compute rather than human knowledge. We introduce Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data, an initial proof-of-concept towards realizing this vision. Our procedure casts synthetic data generation as a search over the space of all computable structure, taking inspiration from Solomonoff induction. Starting from random initialization, two models learn in tandem: a generator proposes programs interpreted by a universal Turing machine, generating byte sequences, while a learner autoregressively predicts these byte sequences. The learner is trained with standard cross-entropy, while the generator is trained with reinforcement learning to produce sequences at the frontier of the learner's capabilities, yielding an adaptive curriculum. A universal Turing machine gives us a search space over all computable data-generating processes, imposing little domain-specific structure, and self-play searches over this space for useful training data. We test whether zero-shot performance on natural data improves predictably with self-play compute; this is a clean test of transfer since neither generator nor learner is trained on natural data. Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss exhibits predictable scaling in compute. The models also exhibit in-context learning, and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training.

</details>


### [203] [PrivDrift: Auditing User-Secret Leakage Under Topic Drift in Active LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30094)
*Luciano Maldonado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrivDrift 基准，用于审计 LLM 在对话场景中用户披露的敏感信息是否会在话题漂移和说服式探测后仍可被行为恢复。通过在 1,000 个受控多轮对话（含种子秘密、高信息密度漂移轮次和标准化提取探针）上测试三个长上下文 LLM，发现对话级混合泄漏率高达 38.7%~54.6%，且额外话题漂移并不能可靠降低泄漏，表明隐私风险应被视为持续的行为失败模式而非单纯的数据记忆或即时越狱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 日益作为面向用户、共享会话和工具增强场景中的持久助手运行，但对话中用户披露的敏感信息即使话题已切换到无关主题，仍可能通过后续提示被行为层恢复。现有评估多聚焦于训练数据记忆或即时越狱，缺乏对"活跃对话中话题漂移后隐私持续泄漏"这一行为失败模式的系统量化，因此需要专门的审计基准。

Method: 作者构建了 PrivDrift 基准，包含 1,000 个受控多轮对话：其中植入（seeded）秘密、设置高内容密度的漂移轮次（topic drift turns），并采用标准化的基于说服（persuasion-based）的提取探针，旨在衡量用户披露的秘密在话题漂移后是否仍可被恢复。该方法以对话级（dialogue-level）的混合泄漏率为核心指标，并在三个具有扩展上下文窗口的 LLM 上进行评估。

Result: 在三个长上下文 LLM 上，对话级混合泄漏率保持在 38.7%~54.6% 的高水平，且随模型、秘密类型和说服强度呈现明显差异。在测试的漂移窗口内，额外的话题漂移并不能可靠地降低泄漏率。

Conclusion: 活跃 LLM 上下文中的隐私风险应被评估为一种持续的行为失败模式，而非仅归因于训练数据记忆或即时越狱行为。PrivDrift 为审计此类持久性隐私泄漏提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as persistent assistants in user-facing, shared-session, and tool-augmented settings. When users disclose sensitive information during an active conversation, that information may remain behaviorally recoverable through later prompts even after the dialogue shifts to unrelated topics. We introduce \textbf{PrivDrift}, a benchmark for auditing whether user-disclosed secrets remain recoverable after conversational topic drift and persuasion-based probing. PrivDrift contains 1{,}000 controlled multi-turn dialogues with seeded secrets, content-dense drift turns, and standardized extraction probes. Across three LLMs with extended context windows, dialogue-level hybrid leakage remains substantial, ranging from 38.7\% to 54.6\%, and varies strongly by model, secret type, and persuasion intensity. Within the tested drift window, additional topic drift does not reliably reduce leakage, suggesting that privacy risk in active LLM contexts should be evaluated as a persistent behavioral failure mode rather than only as training-data memorization or immediate jailbreak behavior.

</details>


### [204] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于假设驱动的仿真工作流（Snowglobe模拟器），用于在部署前筛选客服（CX）智能体。该方法通过合成客户与模拟工具输出，在不调用生产后端的情况下执行多步智能体工作流，并在Nubank的Card Delivery和Card Management智能体上验证。结果显示仿真与生产版本级评价存在高相关性，模拟引导迭代使tNPS提升36.69分，而通过16,000+模拟对话筛选的开放权重模型将自助服务率提高了8.82个百分点，达到Nubank最高水平，且tNPS无显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业中改进CX智能体非常困难：必须检测意图、遵循复杂运营策略并可靠使用工具。手动端到端测试覆盖有限，而真实实验会让客户暴露于失败中，削弱信任。因此需要一种在部署前安全、大规模地筛选候选智能体的方法。

Method: 提出假设驱动的仿真工作流：合成客户对智能体响应做出反应，模拟工具输出以启用多步智能体流程而无需调用生产后端。使用Snowglobe模拟器测试Nubank的Card Delivery智能体及其扩展版本Card Management（巴西Nubank最高流量的聊天支持智能体），并通过仿真筛选开放权重配置（16,000+模拟对话）与评估部署版本。

Result: 4个已部署版本的仿真与生产版本级二元评价得分高度相关。仿真引导迭代使实时A/B测试中tNPS提升36.69分。仿真筛选出的模型在后续实时A/B测试中将自助服务率（SSR）提升8.82个百分点，达到Nubank观察到的最高水平，且tNPS无统计学显著变化。

Conclusion: 仿真使大规模探索模型、推理设置和提示词变得可行而无需让客户暴露，实现了通过实时实验难以达成的生产改进。该工作流可作为CX智能体部署前的有效筛选手段，兼顾覆盖广度与客户信任保护。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [205] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge是一个新基准测试，不给模型问题而是给一堆旧文档，其中隐藏着唯一的逻辑谜题（由SAT求解器在生成时验证唯一性，并通过消融证明每个线索都有用）。25个前沿模型在600多个实例（17,400条记录）上测试，结果发现"直觉"（无问题提示下的任务完成）大幅重塑了排行榜：事实检索的差距仅1.6倍，而直觉表现差距达22倍；第二名事实检索模型在直觉榜上仅排第十四；有模型对是否告知问题无感，甚至有的在无问题条件下的表现显著更好。部分模型被自身内容过滤器阻挡无法到达谜题，因此任何将拒答计为失败的基准实际上是在衡量过滤器行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试都直接给模型问题，无法评估模型在无明确问题指引下的推理能力（即"直觉"）。作者旨在构建一个更接近真实世界的评估范式，测试模型能否从杂乱文档中主动发现并解决隐含的推理任务。

Method: 通过程序化生成包含逻辑谜题的文档堆（信件、收据、日志簿），使用SAT求解器保证解的唯一性并生成消融证明以验证每条线索的必要性；在三种匹配条件下（告知问题vs不告知问题等）对25个前沿模型在600+实例上进行17,400条记录的评估；衡量指标包括"直觉"（仅凭故事完成任务）和"世界重建"两个维度。

Result: 直觉任务下模型间表现差距达22倍，而事实恢复差距仅1.6倍；最佳事实恢复模型在直觉榜上仅排第14；部分模型对是否被告知问题无差异，另一模型无问题时反而显著更好；多个模型因自身内容过滤器在到达谜题前被阻止。

Conclusion: EnigmaForge揭示"直觉推理"是现有模型能力的显著短板，且与传统事实检索能力高度不相关；同时指出了内容过滤器对基准测试有效性的干扰问题，任何将拒答计为失败的基准需重新审视其测量对象。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [206] [GRASP: Generating, Revising, and Assessing for Strategic Planning with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30147)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP，一个策略感知的多阶段规划框架，通过解耦规划流程为三个上下文隔离的模块（GenPlan生成全局宏指导、RevPlan探索局部策略、VerPlan多准则评估），显著提升LLM在复杂任务中生成可执行自然语言计划的能力，并在多任务场景下完全消除性能退化惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在任务复杂度增加时可靠性随之下降，现有直接LLM规划器在复杂任务和多任务场景下性能严重退化，缺乏系统化的策略感知规划框架来应对这一挑战。

Method: GRASP采用策略感知的多阶段规划框架，将规划流程解耦为三个专门化、上下文隔离的模块：(1) GenPlan预编译全局宏观指导方针；(2) RevPlan在隔离的上下文窗口中探索替代局部策略；(3) VerPlan使用多准则判别器独立评估轨迹。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得SOTA效果：Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling提升约12.4%，ZebraLogic提升约30.8%，SciBench Math亦有显著提升。在多任务缩放场景下完全消除退化惩罚，交错双任务环境中比直接LLM规划器绝对准确率提升高达16.7%，并比GPT-5-mini等前沿推理模型高出14.5%。

Conclusion: 通过隔离上下文和强制执行宏观正则化，GRASP能够有效缓解LLM在复杂任务中的性能退化问题，并在多任务场景下实现性能保持，证明了策略感知多阶段规划框架的优越性和通用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) typically exhibit a performance profile where reliability degrades as task complexity increases. We address the challenge of generating high-quality natural language executable plans for complex tasks by introducing $\textbf{GRASP}$, a strategy-aware, multi-stage planning framework. GRASP decouples the planning pipeline across specialized, context-isolated modules: it pre-compiles global macro-guidelines (GenPlan), explores alternative localized strategies within isolated context windows (RevPlan), and independently evaluates trajectories using a multi-criteria discriminator (VerPlan). Empirical evaluations show that GRASP consistently establishes a new state-of-the-art frontier across diverse datasets, yielding substantial accuracy gains over direct LLM planners on Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling ($\sim$12.4$\%$$\uparrow$), ZebraLogic ($\sim$30.8$\%$$\uparrow$), and SciBench Math. Crucially, under multi-task scaling-where standard planners suffer immediate performance collapse-GRASP completely flattens the multi-task degradation penalty. In interleaved dual-task environments, GRASP achieves an absolute accuracy gain of up to 16.7$\%$ over direct LLM planners. Furthermore, by isolating context and enforcing strict macro-regularization, GRASP outperforms frontier reasoning models (such as GPT-5-mini) by a margin of 14.5$\%$.

</details>


### [207] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"蒸馏-强化学习"范式（distill-then-RL）的搜索感知强化学习框架，用于优化查询理解（QU）模型。该框架先用教师-学生监督微调（SFT）构建符合schema的策略初始化，再通过RL阶段从与搜索引擎的实时交互中获取奖励，针对每个QU组件（如意图分类、查询扩展）的运营角色进行组件级优化。在Roblox搜索上，该方法相比SFT策略在NDCG@20上提升8.9个百分点，相比单一端到端奖励训练提升3.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM已将查询理解建模为结构化多任务生成问题（如意图分类、查询扩展），但优化这些模型以产生与搜索引擎耦合的输出仍然困难：静态的、基于标签的监督无法捕捉各组件如何与实际搜索流水线交互并影响下游性能。因此需要一种能够反映组件在搜索系统中真实作用的学习范式。

Method: 提出搜索感知的强化学习框架，采用distill-then-RL两阶段范式：第一阶段通过教师-学生SFT获得格式良好、符合schema的策略初始化；第二阶段用RL优化每个QU组件，奖励来自与搜索引擎的实时交互，并根据各组件的运营角色定制奖励函数，而非使用单一的最终搜索结果奖励。

Result: 在Roblox搜索上的实验表明，组件特定的优化同时提升了各组件自身的效用和下游搜索质量：NDCG@20相比SFT策略提升8.9个百分点，相比使用单一端到端奖励训练的方法提升3.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 组件级奖励（component-specific rewards）相比单一的端到端搜索奖励能更有效地引导QU策略优化，既提升各QU组件的独立性能，也改善整体搜索质量，验证了搜索感知RL框架在查询理解任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [208] [Jev-Mobile: Jev as an Executor for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30186)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-Mobile 提出一种将 VLM 高层规划与轻量级低层动作执行解耦的移动 GUI 智能体范式：VLM 仅低频制定局部目标，通过可访问性树定义结构化动作空间，并由快速决策模型 Jev 高频选择动作。在 AndroidWorld 任务套件上，Jev-Mobile 达到 79% 任务成功率，相比 Step-wise VLM 基线将成功轨迹的平均端到端执行时间降低 32.7%、模型 API 成本降低 73.4%，在保持竞争力的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的移动 GUI 智能体几乎在每个交互步骤都依赖 VLM 同时进行规划与动作落地（action grounding），导致较高的延迟与模型服务成本。作者希望打破这一范式，在不牺牲自适应交互能力的前提下减少昂贵的 VLM 推理调用。

Method: Jev-Mobile 采用"低频 VLM 规划 + 高频轻量执行"的双层架构：VLM 负责制定局部目标；可访问性树（accessibility tree）定义结构化的可执行动作空间；Jev 作为一个快速的类型化决策模型，在该动作空间内反复选择动作。该设计允许在单次 VLM 决策下执行多个 GUI 动作，从而减少昂贵的 VLM 推理频率。

Result: 在完整 AndroidWorld 任务套件上，Jev-Mobile 取得 79% 的任务成功率，对比 SeeAct-V 为 78%、Step-wise VLM 基线为 84%。在成功轨迹中，相对 Step-wise VLM，平均端到端执行时间降低 32.7%，平均模型 API 成本降低 73.4%。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 的高层推理与低层动作执行解耦，能够在大幅提升移动 GUI 智能体效率的同时维持具有竞争力的任务性能，证明了该范式在真实任务场景中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for autonomous mobile GUI agents, but most existing systems rely on the VLM for both planning and action grounding at nearly every interaction step, leading to substantial latency and model-serving cost. We introduce Jev-Mobile, which shifts this paradigm to low-frequency VLM planning and high-frequency lightweight execution: the VLM specifies local goals, the accessibility tree defines a structured executable action space, and Jev, a fast typed decision model, repeatedly selects actions within this space. This design allows multiple GUI actions to be executed under a single VLM decision, reducing expensive VLM inference while preserving adaptive interaction. On the full AndroidWorld task suite, Jev-Mobile achieves 79% task success, compared with 78% for SeeAct-V and 84% for a Step-wise VLM baseline. Among successful trajectories, it reduces mean end-to-end execution time by 32.7% and mean model API cost by 73.4% relative to Step-wise VLM. These results show that decoupling high-level VLM reasoning from low-level action execution can substantially improve mobile GUI agent efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [209] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论视角，揭示稀疏奖励下长程推理中LLM失败源于探索偏差和复合偏差，并据此提出SAGE框架，通过代数稀疏化与双曲结构引导两种互补的结构先验来缓解这两种偏差，在12个基准和7个模型族上优于竞争基线，在Andrews-Curtis问题上取得最高8倍改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理在稀疏奖励机制下仍是LLM的核心挑战，其脆弱性源于复杂推理空间带来的两种偏差：探索偏差（模型倾向于局部看似合理但结构不稳定的分支）和复合偏差（局部微小偏差随深度累积并抑制稀有奖励）。需要理论分析来刻画这些偏差并提供结构先验的设计原则。

Method: 首先提出符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论框架，刻画分支结构与稀疏奖励如何诱发长程推理中的双重偏差。在此基础上提出SAGE框架，包含两个互补的结构引导组件：代数稀疏化（将局部可接受候选投影到算子索引的代数子空间以抑制虚假分支、缓解探索偏差）和双曲结构引导（将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间以提供密集的深度方向信号、缓解复合偏差）。

Result: 在12个基准和7个模型族上，SAGE优于竞争基线；在Andrews-Curtis开放真实世界长程推理问题上实现最高8倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构先验能够有效缓解长程推理中由稀疏奖励引起的探索偏差与复合偏差，SAGE验证了符号闭包分析作为结构先验设计原则的实用价值，为形式化程度较低的长程推理任务提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [210] [ExplorationBench: Measuring AI Systems' Exploration in Verifiable Alien Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30199)
*Ming Zhang,Zhenghao Xiang,Peizhong Gao,Yujiong Shen,Yuhui Wang,Zhonghan Yue,Shihan Dou,Zhangyue Yin,Junjie Ye,Shichun Liu,Weihuang Zheng,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Hongzhang Liu,Jiaqi Shao,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExplorationBench，一个基于可执行的"外星世界"（Alien Worlds）来评估AI科学探索能力的基准框架。该框架通过引入与常识冲突的规则和可精确验证的沙盒环境，解决了探索能力评估中"假设验证"和"是否通过回忆而非探索得出答案"两大难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现始于已知问题的边界之外，AI系统需要具备探索能力（提出假设、设计实验、迭代结果），但评估这种能力非常困难：一是如何验证新假设是否成立，二是如何区分系统是通过真正的探索还是从预训练数据中回忆相关知识的答案。现有基准无法解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出ExplorationBench框架，构建基于可执行规则的外星世界（Alien Worlds）：规则可执行以保证每个答案能精确验证，且规则与熟悉知识冲突以确保仅靠回忆无法解决任务。基准包含两个沙盒：AlienCode（31个发现目标，70个任务）和AlienLogic（31个发现目标，70个任务），每个沙盒提供有缺陷的手册、任务特定的环境反馈和专用工具调用模式。系统需利用这些资源探索沙盒并解决保留的held-out任务。

Result: 评估了10个AI系统，发现最强的系统能够获取和应用不熟悉的规则，但性能在不同轨迹间差异显著，且持续探索可能导致停滞甚至逆转早前的收益。

Conclusion: ExplorationBench是迈向AI系统能够在未知环境中通过探索获取和应用真正新知识的重要一步，为科学探索能力的评估提供了可验证、可复现的框架。

Abstract: Scientific discovery begins where known problems end. There, AI systems must engage in exploration: framing hypotheses, designing experiments, and iterating on the results. However, evaluating this ability is difficult: (1) how to verify whether a genuinely new hypothesis holds, and (2) how to determine whether a system has discovered it through exploration or merely recalled related knowledge from pre-training data. To this end, we introduce ExplorationBench, which turns the wicked problem of evaluating scientific exploration into a concrete and tractable framework built on verifiable Alien Worlds: their rules are executable, so every answer can be checked exactly, and they conflict with familiar knowledge, so recall alone cannot solve the tasks. The benchmark contains two sandboxes, AlienCode (31 discovery targets, 70 tasks) and AlienLogic (24 discovery targets, 70 tasks). Each sandbox provides a flawed manual, task-specific environmental feedback, and a dedicated tool-call schema. Systems use these resources to explore the sandbox, then solve held-out tasks. We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [211] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的框架，可从纵向电子健康记录（EHR）中自动生成问答对，并由19名临床医生验证生成器，构建了可持续维护的评估数据集BRIE（用于检索EHR信息的基准）。在九个LLM和五种推理策略的评估中，最先进的系统经常遗漏临床重要信息，尤其在需要跨文档、跨就诊综合的问题上。BRIE支持静态基准无法实现的多答案评估和持续内容刷新以防数据泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的临床辅助系统正被整合到EHR中，其安全性和实用性取决于严格的评估。然而现有基准为手工整理，更新成本高，且随技术进步迅速过时，亟需一种可持续、可扩展的评估方案。

Method: 提出一个从纵向EHR笔记中自动生成问答对的可扩展框架；由19位临床医生对基准生成器进行人工验证，产出连续可维护的评估数据集BRIE；在九个LLM和五种推理策略上进行系统评估。

Result: 最先进的LLM常常遗漏临床关键信息，尤其在需要跨多个文档和就诊综合信息的问题上表现不佳；生成器本身经过临床验证，使得BRIE能够生成反映临床医生推理差异的多个答案以进行稳健评估，并可持续刷新基准内容以防止数据泄漏。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基准生成能够为在快速演变的医疗环境中部署的临床LLM提供严格且最新的评估，弥补静态基准的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [212] [AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30264)
*Jiabin Qiu,Zixuan Chen,Hongye Cao,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AD-WM，一种用于反事实模型预测控制（MPC）的动作判别联合嵌入世界模型。AD-WM通过残差潜动力学和预测器级别的动作恢复正则化（结合逆动力学和基于条件互信息的归一化恢复目标）来保留规划转移中的动作信息。在OGBench-Cube上，AD-WM将硬起始成功率从3.7%提升到52.0%，在四个模拟环境中超过基线，并在冻结V-JEPA 2编码器的Franka零样本迁移中，将基础拾放任务成功率从42.2%提升到71.1%。结果表明世界模型应保留动作依赖差异以支持反事实选择，而非仅优化事实预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的潜在世界模型被训练来预测事实性转移，而MPC需要从同一状态比较不同候选动作。一个模型即使事实预测误差低，也可能无法区分候选动作，导致控制性能不佳。这揭示了一个根本性矛盾：事实预测准确性并不等同于规划/决策有用性，因此需要引入动作判别性作为世界模型训练的额外目标。

Method: AD-WM采用两个核心设计：1) 残差潜在动力学（residual latent dynamics）来建模转移；2) 预测器级别的动作恢复正则化（predictor-level action-recovery regularization），利用逆动力学和归一化恢复目标（由条件互信息推导）。两个辅助头在测试时被丢弃，不改变MPC推理流程。规划诊断中使用CEM对齐的精英遗憾（elite regret）作为评估指标。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube上，AD-WM将硬起始成功率从3.7%提升至52.0%（相较匹配的LeWM基线）。在五个模拟环境中，四个环境的平均成功率超过复现的基线。在冻结V-JEPA 2编码器和匹配DROID后训练的零样本转移实验中，Franka基础拾放成功率从42.2%提升至71.1%。规划诊断显示事实预测误差和全银行动作排序不跟随闭环成功排序，而CEM对齐的精英遗憾更紧密地追踪成功率。

Conclusion: 世界模型用于规划时应保留动作依赖的差异信息以支持反事实选择，而不是仅仅优化事实预测准确性。AD-WM通过动作判别正则化显著提升了MPC在模拟和真实（零样本迁移）环境中的表现，验证了"可规划性需要动作判别性"这一核心观点。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically trained to predict factual transitions, whereas model predictive control (MPC) must compare alternative actions from the same state. A model can therefore achieve low factual prediction error yet poorly distinguish candidate actions. We introduce AD-WM, an action-discriminative joint-embedding world model for counterfactual MPC. AD-WM combines residual latent dynamics with predictor-level action-recovery regularization, using inverse dynamics and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information. Both objectives encourage planning transitions to preserve action information; their auxiliary heads are discarded at test time, leaving MPC unchanged. On OGBench-Cube, AD-WM improves hard-start success from 3.7% to 52.0% over a matched LeWM baseline and improves mean success over the reproduced baseline in four of five simulation environments. Planning diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not follow the closed-loop success ordering, whereas CEM-aligned elite regret tracks success more closely. With a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder and matched DROID post-training, AD-WM also improves zero-shot transfer to our Franka setup, increasing basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation. These results suggest that world models for planning should preserve action-dependent differences needed for counterfactual selection, rather than optimize factual prediction accuracy alone. More videos and code are available at https://ad-wm.github.io/.

</details>
